180\n    29\t\n    30\t\n    31\tdef get_subjects_and_sessions(data_dir):\n    32\t    \"\"\"Get sorted list of subjects and their sessions.\"\"\"\n    33\t    subjects = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(data_dir)\n    34\t                       if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(data_dir, d)) and d.startswith('jm')])\n    35\t    all_sessions = {}\n    36\t    for subj in subjects:\n    37\t        subj_dir = os.path.join(data_dir, subj)\n    38\t        sessions = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(subj_dir)\n    39\t                          if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(subj_dir, d))])\n    40\t        all_sessions[subj] = sessions\n    41\t    return subjects, all_sessions\n    42\t\n    43\t\n    44\tdef load_session_data(data_dir, subject, session):\n    45\t    \"\"\"Load all data for a single session.\"\"\"\n    46\t    sess_dir = os.path.join(data_dir, subject, session)\n    47\t    s2p_dir = os.path.join(sess_dir, 'suite2p', 'plane0')\n    48\t    move_dir = os.path.join(sess_dir, 'move_deve')\n    49\t\n    50\t    F = np.load(os.path.join(s2p_dir, 'F.npy'))  # (n_neurons, n_frames)\n    51\t    Fneu = np.load(os.path.join(s2p_dir, 'Fneu.npy'))  # (n_neurons, n_frames)\n    52\t    ops = np.load(os.path.join(s2p_dir, 'ops.npy'), allow_pickle=True).item()\n    53\t    me = np.load(os.path.join(move_dir, 'motion_energy_glob.npy'))  # (n_cam_frames,)\n    54\t    tstamps = np.load(os.path.join(move_dir, 'tstamps.npy'))  # (n_cam_frames,)\n    55\t\n    56\t    return F, Fneu, ops, me, tstamps\n    57\t\n    58\t\n    59\tdef compute_dff(F, Fneu, ops):\n    60\t    \"\"\"Compute dF/F using Suite2p's baseline correction.\n    61\t    \n    62\t    Following the paper: \"We used baseline corrected fluorescence traces as our dF/F\n    63\t    (using the default Suite2p parameters)\"\n    64\t    \n    65\t    Steps:\n    66\t    1. Neuropil subtraction: Fc = F - 0.7 * Fneu\n    67\t    2. Baseline correction using maximin method (Suite2p default)\n    68\t    \"\"\"\n    69\t    # Step 1: Neuropil subtraction\n    70\t    Fc = F.copy() - NEUCOEFF * Fneu\n    71\t    \n    72\t    # Step 2: Baseline correction using Suite2p's preprocess function\n    73\t    # This applies the maximin baseline estimation and subtraction\n    74\t    import torch\n    75\t    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')\n    76\t    \n    77\t    dff = preprocess(\n    78\t        F=Fc.copy(),\n    79\t        baseline=ops.get('baseline', 'maximin'),\n    80\t        win_baseline=ops.get('win_baseline', 60.0),\n    81\t        sig_baseline=ops.get('sig_baseline', 10.0),\n    82\t        fs=ops.get('fs', 30.0),\n    83\t        prctile_baseline=ops.get('prctile_baseline', 8.0),\n    84\t        device=device\n    85\t    )\n    86\t    \n    87\t    return dff\n    88\t\n    89\t\n    90\tdef align_motion_energy(me, tstamps, n_neural_frames):\n    91\t    \"\"\"Align motion energy to neural frames, handling missing camera frames.\n    92\t    \n    93\t    When there are missing camera frames (me has fewer frames than neural),\n    94\t    we interpolate the motion energy to match the neural frame count.\n    95\t    \n    96\t    The tstamps array tells us which camera frames correspond to which timepoints.\n    97\t    We use the frame indices implied by tstamps to interpolate.\n    98\t    \"\"\"\n    99\t    n_cam_frames = len(me)\n   100\t    \n   101\t    if n_cam_frames == n_neural_frames:\n   102\t        # No missing frames\n   103\t        return me.astype(np.float64)\n   104\t    \n   105\t    # Missing frames case: interpolate motion energy to neural frame count\n   106\t    # Camera timestamps tell us when each camera frame was acquired\n   107\t    # Neural frames are at regular intervals (1/30 Hz)\n   108\t    # We need to map camera frames to neural frame indices\n   109\t    \n   110\t    # Create neural frame timestamps (regular spacing)\n   111\t    neural_times = np.arange(n_neural_frames) / FRAME_RATE  # in seconds\n   112\t    \n   113\t    # Camera timestamps are in kiloseconds, convert to seconds\n   114\t    cam_times = tstamps * 1000.0  # convert to seconds\n   115\t    \n   116\t    # Interpolate motion energy to neural frame times\n   117\t    me_aligned = np.interp(neural_times, cam_times, me.astype(np.float64))\n   118\t    \n   119\t    return me_aligned\n   120\t\n   121\t\n   122\tdef bin_data(data, bin_size, axis=-1):\n   123\t    \"\"\"Average data in non-overlapping bins along specified axis.\n   124\t    \n   125\t    Following the paper: \"slightly denoised the dF/F as well as the behaviour traces\n   126\t    by averaging in bins of 10 consecutive timestamps\"\n   127\t    \"\"\"\n   128\t    if axis == -1:\n   129\t        axis = data.ndim - 1\n   130\t    \n   131\t    n = data.shape[axis]\n   132\t    n_bins = n // bin_size\n   133\t    # Truncate to multiple of bin_size\n   134\t    slices = [slice(None)] * data.ndim\n   135\t    slices[axis] = slice(0, n_bins * bin_size)\n   136\t    data_trunc = data[tuple(slices)]\n   137\t    \n   138\t    # Reshape and average\n   139\t    new_shape = list(data_trunc.shape)\n   140\t    new_shape[axis] = n_bins\n   141\t    new_shape.insert(axis + 1, bin_size)\n   142\t    data_reshaped = data_trunc.reshape(new_shape)\n   143\t    data_binned = data_reshaped.mean(axis=axis + 1)\n   144\t    \n   145\t    return data_binned\n   146\t\n   147\t\n   148\tdef split_into_trials(data_2d, timepoints_per_trial):\n   149\t    \"\"\"Split a 2D array (n_features x n_timepoints) into trials.\n   150\t    \n   151\t    Returns list of arrays, each (n_features x timepoints_per_trial).\n   152\t    Drops incomplete last trial.\n   153\t    \"\"\"\n   154\t    n_timepoints = data_2d.shape[-1]\n   155\t    n_trials = n_timepoints // timepoints_per_trial\n   156\t    \n   157\t    trials = []\n   158\t    for t in range(n_trials):\n   159\t        start = t * timepoints_per_trial\n   160\t        end = start + timepoints_per_trial\n   161\t        if data_2d.ndim == 2:\n   162\t            trials.append(data_2d[:, start:end].copy())\n   163\t        else:  # 1D\n   164\t            trials.append(data_2d[start:end].copy())\n   165\t    \n   166\t    return trials\n   167\t\n   168\t\n   169\tdef discretize_motion_energy(me_binned_trials, n_bins=5):\n   170\t    \"\"\"Discretize motion energy into equal-percentile bins per session.\n   171\t    \n   172\t    Computes percentile boundaries from ALL trials in the session,\n   173\t    then applies to each trial.\n   174\t    \n   175\t    Returns discretized trials and bin edge info.\n   176\t    \"\"\"\n   177\t    # Concatenate all motion energy values from all trials in this session\n   178\t    all_me = np.concatenate(me_binned_trials)\n   179\t    \n   180\t    # Compute percentile boundaries for equal-frequency bins\n   181\t    percentiles = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)\n   182\t    bin_edges = np.percentile(all_me, percentiles)\n   183\t    \n   184\t    # Make bin edges unique to handle ties\n   185\t    # Use np.digitize with right=False to get bin indices 1..n_bins\n   186\t    # Then subtract 1 to get 0-indexed\n   187\t    discretized_trials = []\n   188\t    for me_trial in me_binned_trials:\n   189\t        # np.digitize returns indices such that bin_edges[i-1] <= x < bin_edges[i]\n   190\t        bin_indices = np.digitize(me_trial, bin_edges[1:-1], right=False)\n   191\t        # Clip to valid range [0, n_bins-1]\n   192\t        bin_indices = np.clip(bin_indices, 0, n_bins - 1)\n   193\t        discretized_trials.append(bin_indices.astype(np.int64))\n   194\t    \n   195\t    return discretized_trials, bin_edges\n   196\t\n   197\t\n   198\tdef process_session(data_dir, subject, session, show_processing=False, session_idx=0):\n   199\t    \"\"\"Process a single session and return trial data.\"\"\"\n   200\t    t0 = time.time()\n   201\t    \n   202\t    # Load data\n   203\t    F, Fneu, ops, me, tstamps = load_session_data(data_dir, subject, session)\n   204\t    n_neurons, n_neural_frames = F.shape\n   205\t    n_cam_frames = len(me)\n   206\t    t_load = time.time() - t0\n   207\t    \n   208\t    print(f\"  [{subject}/{session}] Loaded: {n_neurons} neurons, {n_neural_frames} neural frames, \"\n   209\t          f\"{n_cam_frames} camera frames (load: {t_load:.2f}s)\")\n   210\t    \n   211\t    # Compute dF/F\n   212\t    t1 = time.time()\n   213\t    dff = compute_dff(F, Fneu, ops)\n   214\t    t_dff = time.time() - t1\n   215\t    print(f\"  [{subject}/{session}] dF/F computed ({t_dff:.2f}s), range: [{dff.min():.2f}, {dff.max():.2f}]\")\n   216\t    \n   217\t    # Align motion energy to neural frames\n   218\t    me_aligned = align_motion_energy(me, tstamps, n_neural_frames)\n   219\t    \n   220\t    if n_cam_frames != n_neural_frames:\n   221\t        print(f\"  [{subject}/{session}] Motion energy interpolated: {n_cam_frames} -> {n_neural_frames} frames\")\n   222\t    \n   223\t    # Bin data (10 frames per bin)\n   224\t    t2 = time.time()\n   225\t    dff_binned = bin_data(dff, BIN_SIZE, axis=1)  # (n_neurons, n_bins)\n   226\t    me_binned = bin_data(me_aligned.reshape(1, -1), BIN_SIZE, axis=1).flatten()  # (n_bins,)\n   227\t    t_bin = time.time() - t2\n   228\t    \n   229\t    n_binned = dff_binned.shape[1]\n   230\t    print(f\"  [{subject}/{session}] Binned: {n_binned} timepoints ({t_bin:.2f}s)\")\n   231\t    \n   232\t    # Split into 60-second trials\n   233\t    neural_trials = split_into_trials(dff_binned, TIMEPOINTS_PER_TRIAL)\n   234\t    me_trials = split_into_trials(me_binned, TIMEPOINTS_PER_TRIAL)\n   235\t    n_trials = len(neural_trials)\n   236\t    \n   237\t    print(f\"  [{subject}/{session}] Split into {n_trials} trials of {TRIAL_DURATION_SEC}s \"\n   238\t          f\"({TIMEPOINTS_PER_TRIAL} timepoints each)\")\n   239\t    \n   240\t    # Discretize motion energy (per session)\n   241\t    me_discrete_trials, bin_edges = discretize_motion_energy(me_trials, N_ME_BINS)\n   242\t    \n   243\t    # Create time input for each trial (time elapsed from session start in seconds)\n   244\t    input_trials = []\n   245\t    for t in range(n_trials):\n   246\t        # Time from beginning of session\n   247\t        trial_start_sec = t * TRIAL_DURATION_SEC\n   248\t        time_in_trial = trial_start_sec + np.arange(TIMEPOINTS_PER_TRIAL) / BINNED_RATE\n   249\t        input_trials.append(time_in_trial.astype(np.float32).reshape(1, -1))  # (1, n_timepoints)\n   250\t    \n   251\t    # Format output trials as (1, n_timepoints)\n   252\t    output_trials = [me.astype(np.int64).reshape(1, -1) for me in me_discrete_trials]\n   253\t    \n   254\t    # Convert neural to float32\n   255\t    neural_trials = [n.astype(np.float32) for n in neural_trials]\n   256\t    \n   257\t    t_total = time.time() - t0\n   258\t    print(f\"  [{subject}/{session}] Total processing time: {t_total:.2f}s\")\n   259\t    \n   260\t    # Plotting for --show-processing\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
